# 📚 论文日报 · 翻译智能体 & Agent

本仓库每天 11:00 由本地程序自动更新：抓取 arXiv 关键词论文 + Hugging Face 本周热门论文。

表格里每篇论文有一条大模型根据**摘要**写的筛选笔记，分三段：**做法**、**作者报告**（摘要里的结果和数字，是作者的声称，不是已经证实的结论）、**判断**（一眼能看出的局限，或和我的关系）。筛选笔记只读摘要不读全文，所以它不能代替对证据的核查。

**相关度**：⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ 论文本身就在做我的研究方向；⭐⭐⭐⭐ 不做这些方向，但方法或工具能直接用上；⭐⭐⭐ 有间接借鉴；⭐⭐ 只是同属一个大领域；⭐ 基本无关。

每天另有 8 篇论文的**全文精读**（逐条核查主张与证据），在当天 Issue 的评论里。README 的表格点开可展开英文原摘要。

点右上角 **Watch** → **Custom** → 勾选 **Issues** 可以每天收到邮件通知。

关键词、数量等配置在 [`config.py`](config.py) 里修改。

Last update: 2026-11-28

| **[Stable Gated DeltaNet-2: Understanding and Mitigating Recurrent State Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34051)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34051) | 2026-11-25 | **126** 👍 | **做法**：修 GDN-2 线性注意力在长上下文训练中状态爆炸的问题，用对称擦除和衰减调节写入，理论上给出 O(T) 状态增长上界。<br>**作者报告**：1.6B 参数模型上训练稳定，128K RULER 比 GDN-2 高 6.80 分，九个下游任务平均高 0.62 分。<br>**判断**：可跳过，属模型架构改进，与四个方向无直接关系。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Linear attention offers an efficient solution for long-sequence modeling via linear complexity and fixed-size recurrent states. Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2) introduces independent channel-wise control over erasure and writing, enhancing state-update expressiveness and in-context retrieval, yet it suffers from training failures during long-context training. We trace these failures to the asymmetry between reading and modification directions during erasure, which yields non-normal erase operators whose directional gains accumulate across tokens, triggering exponential state growth even without new writes. To resolve this, we propose , employing symmetric erasure to ensure non-expansive state propagation and decay-regulated residual writing to preserve writing flexibility. Theoretically, we estab</p></details> |
| **[OS-Omni: Benchmarking Generalist Computer-Using Agents on Long-Horizon Workflows Across Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34232)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34232) | 2026-11-25 | **100** 👍 | **做法**：发布跨六平台（Windows/macOS/iOS 等）的 computer-using agent 长流程基准 OS-Omni，含可复现初始化和最终状态评估器。<br>**作者报告**：无效果声称，属基准发布。人类需 67.54 步；最强 agent Claude Opus 5.5 达 68.5%，落后人类参考 8.8 分。<br>**判断**：与 LLM Agent 工具调用评测相关，是长时程多平台能力的现成测试床。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Computer-using agents are increasingly expected to complete realistic user goals rather than isolated GUI actions. We introduce OS-Omni, a live cross-platform benchmark and execution environment for evaluating computer-using agents across Windows, Ubuntu, macOS, iOS, Android, and Web environments. OS-Omni is released in versioned waves of 300 tasks each; this paper focuses on the first release, v1, which contains 48 tasks per platform and 12 cross-platform workflows that pair a desktop with an Android phone. OS-Omni provides reproducible initializat</p></details> |
| **[Rewirable Circuits in Transformers with Loom Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30634)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.30634) | 2026-11-23 | **93** 👍 | **做法**：提出 loom attention，让 Transformer 头可以关注此前头的输出，形成双流架构 LoomFormer。<br>**作者报告**：同等计算预算下匹配3倍计算的 Transformer；LoomPythia-2.8B 在300B token 训练后0/5-shot 匹配或超过 Pythia-12B。<br>**判断**：可跳过：架构创新，未涉及我的方向。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>We propose loom attention, which allows a Transformer to dynamically rewire its circuits at every token by interweaving a head's vertical attention over the outputs of earlier heads (to obtain its query, key and value) with its horizontal attention over tokens. We instantiate vertical attention efficiently with normalized and gated linear attention over a per-token matrix memory, which stores the compressed head outputs and serves as a second residual stream. This yields LoomFormer, a dual-stream architecture that splits control and content between the vector and matrix streams. Across model sizes and training token budgets in language modeling and downstream tasks, LoomFormer matches Transformers trained with over 3x compute and recent residual-stream baselines (HC/mHC, MUDDFormer, AttnRe</p></details> |
| **[Sage-Med: Scaling Medical Multimodal Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34575)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34575) | 2026-11-25 | **81** 👍 | **做法**：研究医学多模态大模型的scaling规律，用322.7M候选记录分阶段筛选加渐进训练课程，训练出35B参数（激活3B）的Sage-Med-Preview。<br>**作者报告**：在238个临床任务上平均58.26%（31个公开多模态benchmark），超过Hulu-Med-235B-A22B（50.99%）和Intern-S2-Preview（52.24%），接近GPT-6.2-Sol。<br>**判断**：纯医学多模态模型报告，与生物智能体的实验规划自动化无关。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Establishing effective scaling laws for medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remains a persistent and fundamental challenge. | **[Mixture-of-Loops Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34923)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.34923) | 2026-11-25 | **74** 👍 | **做法**：改进Looped Transformer，用KV作为跨层跨循环记忆的Mixture-of-Loops注意力，并提出U形MoLT分离记忆构建与预测以跳过无用遍历。<br>**作者报告**：400B token预训练下，700M模型CE token效率1.7倍，3B MoE十域困惑度降0.75、十任务精度升2.1个百分点；延迟与OLMo3相当。<br>**判断**：底层架构工作，与翻译/生物/教学/工具调用均无直接关系。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Looped Transformers boost expressivity by reusing shared layers across loops, yet at the cost of efficiency. Under loop residual, MoLT-R retains the residual stream and reads local KV across loops for expressivity. Under loop KV, MoLT-KV instead restarts from embeddings and reads current-loop local KV with preceding-loop memory KV at earlier positions for efficiency. MoLT-KV eliminates same-position cross-loop dependence and, paired with loop forcing for parallel tr</p></details> |
| **[ACAP: Asynchronous Coding Agents for Efficient Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31205)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.31205) | 2026-11-23 | **67** 👍 | **做法**：ACAP：把一个机器人拆成多个持久异步 coding agent 分管不同控制组，加超 agent 协调物理依赖。<br>**作者报告**：72次真实滚动中任务均衡时间成本降28.2%；八个协作设置低于固定委派；附90次滚动研究和数据集。<br>**判断**：多 agent 异步分工与协调思路可借鉴，场景是机器人。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Coding agents can construct and revise robot behavior during execution, but using one agent for the whole robot can delay otherwise independent work. However, different robot components often have physical dependencies that require coordination. To address this challenge, ACAP introduces a hyper-agent that assigns tasks, coordinates dependencies, and dynamically allocates control between the lead and member agents. This allows independent work to progress concurrently while supporting t</p></details> |
| **[Antom 3-in-1 Transformer: A Payment Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25403)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25403) | 2026-11-19 | **67** 👍 | **做法**：Antom 约7B参数支付风控基础模型，融合序列、表格、图三种表示，先预训练再迁移到各业务。<br>**作者报告**：迁移实验中目标业务指标比从零训练提升34.5%；25%标签即超过全标签基线；线上实验整体损失率相对 XGB-Online 降37.88%。<br>**判断**：可跳过：支付风控领域模型，方法难直接迁移到我的方向。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>We report the development of Antom 3-in-1 Transformer, a payment foundation model that integrates sequence, tabular, and graph representations for payment risk detection. In a cross-business transfer experiment, pretraining improves the target business metric by 34.5% over the same architecture trained from scratch. With only 25% of the training labels, the pretrained model outperforms the full-label scratch baseline in Average Precision (AP) and Recall@0.5% on different merchant domains. In a four-month online experiment on Ant Internation</p></details> |
| **[Lazy Agents Answer Against Their Own Beliefs: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Beliefs Teaches Them to Act on What They Believe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32884)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32884) | 2026-11-24 | **65** 👍 | **做法**：RLVB：利用模拟器可算的后验作奖励，奖励在证据不足时继续行动、证据足够时提交，治 agent 过早回答的懒惰行为。<br>**作者报告**：三个 VLM 在九个设置平均提升17.1%；GRPO/PPO 平均低于基模型；比 LLM 裁判的过程奖励 PPO 高9.3%且省1.6倍 GPU 时间；充足证据下作答比例大增。<br>**判断**：agent 训练方法与 Pro Worlds 基准对 LLM Agent 研究有借鉴。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>AI agents working on long tasks often commit to an answer before they have enough evidence, a behavior called laziness. In our evaluations, they frequently commit even while their stated belief still names several possible answers. An outcome reward at the end of the episode scores only the final answer, so it cannot teach the agent when to commit, because a lucky guess receives the same reward as an answer backed by evidence. During training, however, the simulator can compute the exact posterior over the answer at every step and tell whether the evidence is already enough. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Beliefs (RLVB), which rewards the agent for taking further steps while this posterior still allows several answers and for committing once the evidence is sufficient. T</p></details> |
| **[Don't Sleep, Keep Watch: Proactive Perception for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28947)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.28947) | 2026-11-21 | **65** 👍 | **做法**：NightWatch：给计算机使用 agent 配0.8B小模型做流式界面监控，弥补快照式感知的滞后。<br>**作者报告**：OSWorld 非推理时间降33%、浪费时间降53%且成功率持平；DynaCU-Bench 成功率从39.5%升到44.2%；比30倍大的决策模型更高效。<br>**判断**：Agent 感知时机问题的解法可借鉴到我的 agent 工作。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Computer-use agents (CUAs) built on vision-language models are increasingly entrusted with long-horizon tasks in live desktop, web, and mobile environments, where the screen keeps changing on its own. However, current CUAs lack real-time situational awareness: they perceive the environment through discrete snapshots, and the substantial inference latency of VLMs, compounded by highly dynamic interfaces, opens a perception-action gap in which the observed state frequently becomes stale before the corresponding action is executed. To bridge this gap, we present NightWatch, a plug-and-play framework that endows any CUA with continuous, temporally grounded perception without changing the agent's model, prompt, or action space. NightWatch couples the VLM agent with NightWatch-0.8B, a lightweigh</p></details> |

| **[Doc2FRC: Length-Consistent Document-Level Machine Translation via Fixed-Range Chunking](http://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12674v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.12674v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>To appear in Proceedings of the Eleventh Conference on Machine Translation (WMT2026)</p></details> | 2026-09-11 | **做法**：提出Fixed-Range Chunking，用动态规划把文档切成定长区间块，训练推理一致以消除长度分布失配。<br>**作者报告**：7B LLM上FRC微调优于直接Doc2Doc微调，超IWSLT2017已有方法；10语言GlobVDoc测试上提升分布外翻译；摘要未给具体数字。<br>**判断**：文档级翻译分块方法，与方向直接相关，5星。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Advanced large language models (LLMs) with long context windows can substantially reduce input truncation in document-level machine translation (DocMT). However, direct Doc2Doc translation remains prone to n-gram repetition and progressive quality degradation. A common remedy is to segment the document into finer-grained chunks. Nonetheless, conventional rule-based chunking approaches fail to handle the length distribution mismatch between training and inference. | **[AppSci-Bench: Can LLMs Build AI Agents and Make Them Better?](http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25360v2)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25360v2) | 2026-11-21 | **做法**：构建 AppSci-Bench，评测 LLM 能否像应用科学家一样为企业构建并改进智能体：需求引导、基础设施探索、实验设计。<br>**作者报告**：七个前沿模型表现弱，最好的 Claude Opus 5.0 三次构建平均仅 59.1%；模型常自评饱和但解不出 held-out 任务。<br>**判断**：4 星：直接评 LLM 构建 agent 的能力，含 Slides Generation 域，与方向④和①有交集。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used not only to power AI agents but also to build them. | **[InfoVis: Unified Slide Image Generation and Editing](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16784v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16784v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>Code: https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/InfoVis</p></details> | 2026-10-12 | **做法**：统一9B幻灯片图像生成与编辑模型InfoVis，配13M+1.9M训练数据和VisSlideBench评测。<br>**作者报告**：开源模型中全11项指标领先，总分7.94对Nano-Banana-2的7.99；后训练提升1.6分。<br>**判断**：视觉生成为主，翻译场景复用有限，可跳过：非翻译任务。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Generating and editing presentation slides requires models to combine accurate text rendering, instruction following, visual grounding, aesthetic design, and precise editing within one canvas. Its automatic rankings agree with blinded, dimension-wise human rankings. Using these resources, we train InfoVis, a unified 9B slide generation and editing model.</p></details> |

| **[CommitKV: Commit-Aligned KV Cache Compression for Multimodal Agents](http://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07855v3)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.07855v3) | 2026-11-24 | **做法**：多模态智能体工具调用累积观察导致 KV cache 膨胀，CommitKV 免训练压缩：对比工具调用前后同一页 token 的影响再决定淘汰<br>**作者报告**：Qwen3-VL-8B 在 512-token 预算下 69.85%，比 FullKV 低 0.91%；256 tokens 下超最强基线 8.44%；GPU 峰值内存降 54.37%-55.23%，端到端推理加速 2.12-2.17 倍<br>**判断**：不做我的方向，但做 Agent 工具调用的长观察场景降本，方法可直接用于 LLM Agent 部署<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Multimodal agents gather visual evidence through reasoning and tool use, extending their ability to solve tasks beyond the initial input. When observations shift attention, they may mistake temporarily reduced influence for lost usefulness and evict information needed later. | **[TrustMI: Causally controlling how assistants trust their users](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06064v2)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.06064v2) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>34 pages, 16 figures, 12 tables</p></details> | 2026-11-24 | **做法**：定义助手对用户的信任为接受脆弱性的意愿，从 2,000 对比对对话学转向矩阵，因果控制信任决策。<br>**作者报告**：六个指令模型上转向可单调改变信任决策；效应扩展到有害请求、提示注入等安全场景；未给量化数字。<br>**判断**：Agent 安全的表示层面的研究，可跳过。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Large Language Model (LLM) assistants routinely decide whether they can trust users and third parties whose competence, intentions, and integrity they cannot verify. | **[Robo-Harness K1: Harnessing Robot-Use Agents via Perception Augmentation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29389v2)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.29389v2) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>preprint v2</p></details> | 2026-11-24 | **做法**：把区域定位、深度几何、视觉锚点等做成可调用感知工具，VLM 调用它们测量场景并选动作。<br>**作者报告**：LIBERO-PRO 上把 Gemini 3.7 Flash 从 16.7% 提到 71.7%，GPT-6 Astra 从 63.3% 到 93.3%；蒸馏的 Qwen3.5-9B 超同数据 OpenVLA。<br>**判断**：机器人方向，可跳过。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Vision-language models (VLMs) understand manipulation scenes and instructions, yet as robot-use agents they struggle to turn 2D observations into the 3D relations that motion requires. | **[Urbex: Agentic Spatial Grounding for City-Scale 3D Scenes](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18356v2)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18356v2) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>NeurIPS 2026. 23 pages, 10 figures. Code: https://github.com/AMAP-ML/Urbex</p></details> | 2026-11-24 | **做法**：Urbex 把城市级 3D 定位改表述为主动空间搜索，VLM Agent 查询地标、缩放、渲染斜视图后提交框，RL 优化。<br>**作者报告**：CityRefer 上 Hit@1 与 IoU 最佳，无需候选框，比最强排序基线快得多；STPLS3D-Refer 零样本更鲁棒；未给具体数字。<br>**判断**：空间推理 Agent，可跳过。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>City-scale 3D grounding is commonly formulated as candidate ranking, which requires precomputed instance proposals and becomes inefficient in large outdoor scenes. We propose Urbex, an agentic vision-language framework that instead casts grounding as active spatial search. Urbex represents a city-scale 3D scene as an interactive multi-view environment, allowing a VLM agent to query landmarks, zoom into local regions, render oblique views, and commit a grounded bounding box. We optimize this tool-use policy with reinforcement learning on CityRefer, using smooth localization rewards and lightweight shaping rewards for efficient exploration.
